import './globals.css';
import { LanguageSwitcher } from '@/components/LanguageSwitcher';
import type { Metadata, Viewport } from 'next';
import {
  Cormorant_Garamond,
  Great_Vibes,
  Montserrat,
} from 'next/font/google';

const cormorant = Cormorant_Garamond({
  subsets: ['latin'],
  weight: ['300', '400', '500', '600', '700'],
  variable: '--font-cormorant',
  display: 'swap',
});

const greatVibes = Great_Vibes({
  subsets: ['latin'],
  weight: ['400'],
  variable: '--font-great-vibes',
  display: 'swap',
});

const montserrat = Montserrat({
  subsets: ['latin'],
  weight: ['300', '400', '500', '600'],
  variable: '--font-montserrat',
  display: 'swap',
});

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  themeColor: '#fbf8f0',
};

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  manifest: '/manifest.webmanifest',

  icons: {
    icon: [
      {
        url: '/icons/wedding-app-icon.png',
        type: 'image/png',
        sizes: '1024x1024',
      },
    ],
    apple: [
      {
        url: '/icons/wedding-app-icon.png',
        type: 'image/png',
        sizes: '1024x1024',
      },
    ],
  },

  appleWebApp: {
    capable: true,
    statusBarStyle: 'default',
    title: 'Betslot & Amanuel',
  },

  title: "Betslot & Amanuel — We're Getting Married!",

  description:
    'Join us in celebrating the wedding of Betslot Andualem Demissie and Amanuel Tamiru Gebre on 19 November 2028 at Friendship Park, Addis Ababa, Ethiopia.',

  openGraph: {
    title: "Betslot & Amanuel — We're Getting Married!",

    images: [
      {
        url: '/icons/wedding-app-icon.png',
        width: 1024,
        height: 1024,
        alt: 'Betslot and Amanuel Wedding',
      },
    ],
  },

  twitter: {
    card: 'summary_large_image',
    images: ['/icons/wedding-app-icon.png'],
  },
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <html
      lang="en"
      className={`${cormorant.variable} ${greatVibes.variable} ${montserrat.variable}`}
    >
      <body className="font-body antialiased">

      </body>

}

========== FILE: app\page.tsx ==========
'use client';

import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Envelope } from '@/components/Envelope';
import { MusicPlayer } from '@/components/MusicPlayer';
import { HeroSection, CountdownSection } from '@/components/HeroSection';
import { OurStorySection } from '@/components/sections/OurStorySection';
import { LocationSection } from '@/components/sections/LocationSection';
import { ScheduleSection } from '@/components/sections/ScheduleSection';
import { DressCodeSection } from '@/components/sections/DressCodeSection';
import { AccommodationSection } from '@/components/sections/AccommodationSection';
import { GiftSection } from '@/components/sections/GiftSection';
import { FAQSection } from '@/components/sections/FAQSection';
import { PersonalMessageSection } from '@/components/sections/PersonalMessageSection';
import { RSVPSection } from '@/components/RSVPSection';
import { Footer } from '@/components/Footer';
import { FallingPetals } from '@/components/FloralDecorations';
import { CoupleWatermarks } from '@/components/CoupleWatermarks';

export default function Home() {
  const [opened, setOpened] = useState(false);
  const rsvpRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const scrollToRSVP = () => {
    const el = document.getElementById('rsvp');
    if (el) {
      el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="relative min-h-screen overflow-x-hidden">
      <CoupleWatermarks />
      {/* Envelope opening screen */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {!opened && <Envelope onOpen={() => setOpened(true)} />}
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Main content — revealed after envelope opens */}
      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
        animate={{ opacity: opened ? 1 : 0 }}
        transition={{ duration: 1, delay: 0.3 }}
        className={`relative z-10 ${opened ? '' : 'pointer-events-none'}`}
      >
        {/* Floating petals over content */}
        <FallingPetals count={8} />

        {/* Hero with names, date, photo */}
        <HeroSection onRSVP={scrollToRSVP} />

        {/* Countdown */}
        <CountdownSection />

        {/* Our Story */}
        <OurStorySection />

        {/* Wedding Location */}
        <LocationSection />

        {/* Wedding Schedule */}
        <ScheduleSection />

        {/* Dress Code */}
        <DressCodeSection />

        {/* Accommodation */}
        <AccommodationSection />

        {/* Gift Information */}
        <GiftSection />

        {/* RSVP */}
        <RSVPSection />

        {/* FAQ */}
        <FAQSection />

        {/* Personal Message */}
        <PersonalMessageSection />

        {/* Footer */}
        <Footer />
      </motion.div>

      {/* Music player (always visible) */}
      <MusicPlayer />
    </main>
  );
}


========== FILE: app\admin\page.tsx ==========

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { Lock, Loader2, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { FloralSpray, FloralDivider } from "@/components/FloralDecorations";

export default function AdminLoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [checkingSession, setCheckingSession] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    const checkSession = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch("/api/admin/me", {
          method: "GET",
          cache: "no-store",
        });

        if (!active) return;

        if (response.ok) {
          router.replace("/admin/dashboard");
          return;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Session check failed:", err);
      }

      if (active) {
        setCheckingSession(false);
      }
    };

    checkSession();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [router]);

  const handleLogin = async (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/admin/login", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
      });

      const result = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          result.error || "Invalid credentials. Please try again."
        );
      }

      router.replace("/admin/dashboard");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to sign in. Please try again."
      );
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (checkingSession) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-ivory">
        <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-blush-deep animate-spin" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center relative overflow-hidden">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 watercolor-texture" />

      <FloralSpray className="absolute top-8 left-4 w-28 h-28 opacity-50" />
      <FloralSpray className="absolute top-8 right-4 w-28 h-28 opacity-50" flip />
      <FloralSpray className="absolute bottom-8 left-4 w-28 h-28 opacity-50" />
      <FloralSpray className="absolute bottom-8 right-4 w-28 h-28 opacity-50" flip />

      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        transition={{ duration: 0.8 }}
        className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-md mx-auto px-6"
      >
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-ivory to-blush-light/40 rounded-3xl p-8 md:p-10 border border-gold/30 shadow-xl">
          <div className="text-center mb-8">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-blush-light border border-blush-deep/30 mb-4">
              <Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-blush-deep" />
            </div>

            <h1 className="font-script text-4xl text-blush-deep">
              Admin Login
            </h1>

            <p className="font-body text-xs tracking-[0.3em] uppercase text-ink-light mt-2">
              Betslot &amp; Amanuel Wedding
            </p>

            <FloralDivider className="mt-4" />
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-5">
            <div>
              <label className="block font-body text-sm text-ink mb-1.5">
                Email
              </label>

              <input
                type="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                required
                placeholder="admin@email.com"
                autoComplete="username"
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/30 bg-ivory px-4 py-2.5 font-body text-sm text-ink focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blush-deep/30"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block font-body text-sm text-ink mb-1.5">
                Password
              </label>

              <div className="relative">
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  required
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  autoComplete="current-password"
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/30 bg-ivory px-4 py-2.5 pr-10 font-body text-sm text-ink focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blush-deep/30"
                />

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  aria-label={showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}
                  className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-light hover:text-blush-deep"
                >
                  {showPassword ? (
                    <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" />
                  ) : (
                    <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-50 border border-red-200 px-4 py-3">
                <p className="font-body text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blush-deep to-blush-deep/85 text-ivory font-body text-sm tracking-[0.2em] uppercase shadow-lg disabled:opacity-60 hover:shadow-xl transition-shadow flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
                  Signing in...
                </>
              ) : (
                "Sign In"
              )}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="mt-6 text-center">
            <a
              href="/"
              className="font-body text-xs text-ink-light hover:text-blush-deep transition-colors"
            >
              ← Back to invitation
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </div>
  );
}


========== FILE: app\admin\dashboard\page.tsx ==========
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import {
  LayoutDashboard, Users, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Clock, UserCheck,
  Search, Download, LogOut, Edit2, Trash2, X, Mail, Phone, Calendar,
  Plus, Shield, ChevronDown,
} from "lucide-react";

interface RSVPGuest {
  id: string;
  full_name: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string | null;
  attending: boolean;
  num_guests: number;
  accompanying_names: string | null;
  dietary_requirements: string | null;
  message: string | null;
  edit_token: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

interface AdminUser {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  email: string;
  full_name: string;
  role: string;
}

type FilterType = "all" | "attending" | "declined";

async function apiRequest(url: string, options: RequestInit = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    ...options,
    cache: "no-store",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...options.headers,
    },
  });

  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(result.error || "The request could not be completed.");
  }

  return result;
}

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [adminUser, setAdminUser] = useState<AdminUser | null>(null);
  const [guests, setGuests] = useState<RSVPGuest[]>([]);
  const [allStaff, setAllStaff] = useState<AdminUser[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<FilterType>("all");
  const [editingGuest, setEditingGuest] = useState<RSVPGuest | null>(null);
  const [showStaffPanel, setShowStaffPanel] = useState(false);
  const [newStaffEmail, setNewStaffEmail] = useState("");
  const [newStaffName, setNewStaffName] = useState("");
  const [newStaffPassword, setNewStaffPassword] = useState("");
  const [staffError, setStaffError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [staffSuccess, setStaffSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [actionLoading, setActionLoading] = useState(false);
  const [pageError, setPageError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
    const me = await apiRequest("/api/admin/me");

    if (!me.authenticated) {
      router.replace("/admin");
      return;
    }

    setAdminUser({
      id: "",
      user_id: "",
      email: "",
      full_name: me.admin.full_name,
      role: me.admin.role,
    });

    const data = await apiRequest("/api/admin/dashboard");

    setGuests(data.guests);
    setAllStaff(data.staff || []);
  }, [router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function initialize() {
      try {
        await loadDashboard();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Dashboard initialization failed:", error);
        if (active) {
          setPageError(
            error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unable to load dashboard."
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    initialize();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const refreshGuests = async () => {
    const data = await apiRequest("/api/admin/dashboard");
    setGuests(data.guests);
    setAllStaff(data.staff || []);
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    try {
      await apiRequest("/api/admin/logout", { method: "POST" });
    } finally {
      router.replace("/admin");
    }
  };

  const stats = {
    total: guests.length,
    confirmed: guests.filter((guest) => guest.attending).length,
    declined: guests.filter((guest) => !guest.attending).length,
    pending: 0,
    totalAttendees: guests
      .filter((guest) => guest.attending)
      .reduce((sum, guest) => sum + 1 + guest.num_guests, 0),
  };

  const filteredGuests = guests.filter((guest) => {
    const term = search.toLowerCase();

    const matchesSearch =
      !term ||
      guest.full_name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      guest.phone.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
      (guest.email || "").toLowerCase().includes(term);

    const matchesFilter =
      filter === "all" ||
      (filter === "attending" && guest.attending) ||
      (filter === "declined" && !guest.attending);

    return matchesSearch && matchesFilter;
  });

  const exportCSV = () => {
    const headers = [
      "Name", "Phone", "Email", "Attending", "Num Guests",
      "Accompanying Names", "Dietary Requirements", "Message",
      "Submitted At", "Updated At",
    ];

    const rows = guests.map((guest) => [
      guest.full_name,
      guest.phone,
      guest.email || "",
      guest.attending ? "Yes" : "No",
      guest.num_guests,
      guest.accompanying_names || "",
      guest.dietary_requirements || "",
      guest.message || "",
      new Date(guest.created_at).toLocaleString(),
      new Date(guest.updated_at).toLocaleString(),
    ]);

    const csv = [headers, ...rows]
      .map((row) =>
        row.map((cell) => `"${String(cell).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(",")
      )
      .join("\n");

    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");

    link.href = url;
    link.download = `wedding-guests-${new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]}.csv`;
    link.click();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleUpdateGuest = async (updated: Partial<RSVPGuest>) => {
    if (!editingGuest) return;

    setActionLoading(true);
    setStaffError(null);

    try {
      await apiRequest("/api/admin/dashboard", {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: editingGuest.id, ...updated }),
      });

      setEditingGuest(null);
      await refreshGuests();
    } catch (error) {
      setStaffError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to update guest."
      );
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteGuest = async (guest: RSVPGuest) => {
    if (!confirm(`Remove ${guest.full_name}'s response? This cannot be undone.`)) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      await apiRequest("/api/admin/dashboard", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ action: "delete_guest", id: guest.id }),
      });
      await refreshGuests();
    } catch (error) {
      setStaffError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to remove guest."
      );
    }
  };

  const handleAddStaff = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setActionLoading(true);
    setStaffError(null);
    setStaffSuccess(null);

    try {
      await apiRequest("/api/admin/dashboard", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: "add_staff",
          email: newStaffEmail,
          full_name: newStaffName,
          password: newStaffPassword,
        }),
      });

      setStaffSuccess(`${newStaffName} has been added as an admin staff member.`);
      setNewStaffEmail("");
      setNewStaffName("");
      setNewStaffPassword("");
      await refreshGuests();
    } catch (error) {
      setStaffError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to add staff member."
      );
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveStaff = async (staff: AdminUser) => {
    if (staff.role === "owner") {
      setStaffError("Cannot remove the owner account.");
      return;
    }

    if (!confirm(`Remove ${staff.full_name} from admin staff?`)) return;

    setStaffError(null);

    try {
      await apiRequest("/api/admin/dashboard", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          action: "remove_staff",
          id: staff.id,
          user_id: staff.user_id,
        }),
      });
      await refreshGuests();
    } catch (error) {
      setStaffError(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Failed to remove staff member."
      );
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-ivory">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-blush-deep border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-3" />
          <p className="font-body text-sm text-ink-light">Loading dashboard...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (pageError) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-ivory p-6">
        <div className="max-w-md text-center">
          <p className="font-serif-display text-2xl text-ink mb-3">
            Dashboard unavailable
          </p>
          <p className="font-body text-sm text-red-600 mb-5">{pageError}</p>
          <button
            onClick={() => router.replace("/admin")}
            className="rounded-full bg-blush-deep text-ivory px-6 py-3"
          >
            Return to Admin Login
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-ivory to-blush-light/30">
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 bg-ivory/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-blush-deep/15">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blush-deep/10 flex items-center justify-center">
              <LayoutDashboard className="w-5 h-5 text-blush-deep" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="font-serif-display text-xl text-ink">Wedding Dashboard</h1>
              <p className="font-body text-xs text-ink-light">
                Betslot &amp; Amanuel · {adminUser?.full_name}
                {adminUser?.role === "owner" && " · Owner"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {adminUser?.role === "owner" && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowStaffPanel(!showStaffPanel)}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-blush-light border border-blush-deep/20 text-blush-deep font-body text-xs"
              >
                <Shield className="w-4 h-4" /> Staff
              </button>
            )}
            <a
              href="/"
              className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-ivory border border-blush-deep/20 text-ink font-body text-xs"
            >
              View Site
            </a>
            <button
              onClick={handleLogout}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-ivory border border-blush-deep/20 text-ink font-body text-xs"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" /> Logout
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 md:px-6 py-8">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4 mb-8">
          <StatCard icon={<Users />} label="Total Responses" value={stats.total} color="blush" />
          <StatCard icon={<CheckCircle2 />} label="Confirmed" value={stats.confirmed} color="green" />
          <StatCard icon={<XCircle />} label="Declined" value={stats.declined} color="red" />
          <StatCard icon={<Clock />} label="Pending" value={stats.pending} color="amber" />
          <StatCard icon={<UserCheck />} label="Expected Attendees" value={stats.totalAttendees} color="blue" />
        </div>

        <AnimatePresence>
          {showStaffPanel && adminUser?.role === "owner" && (
            <motion.section
              initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, height: "auto" }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
              className="mb-8 overflow-hidden"
            >
              <div className="bg-ivory rounded-2xl border border-gold/20 p-6 shadow-sm">
                <h2 className="font-serif-display text-xl text-ink mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Shield className="w-5 h-5 text-blush-deep" />
                  Admin Staff Management
                </h2>

                <form onSubmit={handleAddStaff} className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-6">
                  <input
                    placeholder="Full name"
                    value={newStaffName}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewStaffName(e.target.value)}
                    required
                    className="rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  />
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="Email address"
                    value={newStaffEmail}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewStaffEmail(e.target.value)}
                    required
                    className="rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  />
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Temporary password (8+ characters)"
                    value={newStaffPassword}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewStaffPassword(e.target.value)}
                    required
                    minLength={8}
                    className="rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                  />
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={actionLoading}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-blush-deep text-ivory text-sm py-2 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Add Staff
                  </button>
                </form>

                {staffError && <p className="text-sm text-red-600 mb-3">{staffError}</p>}
                {staffSuccess && <p className="text-sm text-green-600 mb-3">{staffSuccess}</p>}

                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {allStaff.map((staff) => (
                    <div
                      key={staff.id}
                      className="flex items-center justify-between bg-blush-light/40 rounded-lg px-4 py-3"
                    >
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm text-ink">{staff.full_name}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-ink-light">{staff.email}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-full ${
                          staff.role === "owner"
                            ? "bg-gold/20 text-gold"
                            : "bg-blush-deep/10 text-blush-deep"
                        }`}>
                          {staff.role}
                        </span>
                        {staff.role !== "owner" && (
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleRemoveStaff(staff)}
                            className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600"
                            aria-label={`Remove ${staff.full_name}`}
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.section>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>

        <section className="bg-ivory rounded-2xl border border-gold/20 p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6">
            <h2 className="font-serif-display text-xl text-ink">Guest List</h2>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <div className="relative">
                <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-ink-light absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                <input
                  placeholder="Search guests..."
                  value={search}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-sm w-full md:w-56"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="relative">
                <select
                  value={filter}
                  onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value as FilterType)}
                  className="appearance-none rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory pl-3 pr-8 py-2 text-sm"
                >
                  <option value="all">All</option>
                  <option value="attending">Attending</option>
                  <option value="declined">Declined</option>
                </select>
                <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={exportCSV}
                disabled={!guests.length}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-blush-deep text-ivory text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <Download className="w-4 h-4" /> Export CSV
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {filteredGuests.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-12">
              <Users className="w-12 h-12 text-blush-deep/30 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <p className="text-sm text-ink-light">
                {guests.length === 0
                  ? "No RSVP responses yet. Responses will appear here once guests submit them."
                  : "No guests match your search."}
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-blush-deep/15">
                    {["Name", "Contact", "Status", "Guests", "Dietary", "Submitted", "Actions"].map((heading, index) => (
                      <th
                        key={heading}
                        className={`text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-light py-3 px-2 ${
                          (index === 1 || index === 3) ? "hidden md:table-cell" : ""
                        } ${index === 4 || index === 5 ? "hidden lg:table-cell" : ""} ${
                          index === 6 ? "text-right" : ""
                        }`}
                      >
                        {heading}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {filteredGuests.map((guest) => (
                    <tr key={guest.id} className="border-b border-blush-deep/8 hover:bg-blush-light/20">
                      <td className="py-3 px-2">
                        <p className="text-sm text-ink font-medium">{guest.full_name}</p>
                        {guest.message && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-ink-light italic mt-0.5 max-w-xs truncate">
                            “{guest.message}”
                          </p>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 hidden md:table-cell">
                        <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 text-xs text-ink-light">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Phone className="w-3 h-3" />{guest.phone}</span>
                          {guest.email && <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Mail className="w-3 h-3" />{guest.email}</span>}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2">
                        <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs ${
                          guest.attending ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700" : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                        }`}>
                          {guest.attending ? <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> : <XCircle className="w-3 h-3" />}
                          {guest.attending ? "Attending" : "Declined"}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 hidden md:table-cell">
                        <span className="text-sm text-ink">
                          {guest.attending ? `${1 + guest.num_guests} total` : "—"}
                        </span>
                        {guest.accompanying_names && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-ink-light mt-0.5 max-w-xs truncate">{guest.accompanying_names}</p>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 hidden lg:table-cell text-xs text-ink-light">
                        {guest.dietary_requirements || "—"}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 hidden lg:table-cell text-xs text-ink-light">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Calendar className="w-3 h-3" />
                          {new Date(guest.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setEditingGuest(guest)}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-blush-deep hover:bg-blush-deep/10"
                            aria-label={`Edit ${guest.full_name}`}
                          >
                            <Edit2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                          </button>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleDeleteGuest(guest)}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50"
                            aria-label={`Delete ${guest.full_name}`}
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {editingGuest && (
          <EditGuestModal
            guest={editingGuest}
            onSave={handleUpdateGuest}
            onClose={() => setEditingGuest(null)}
            loading={actionLoading}
          />
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatCard({
  icon, label, value, color,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: number;
  color: "blush" | "green" | "red" | "amber" | "blue";
}) {
  const colors = {
    blush: "bg-blush-deep/10 text-blush-deep",
    green: "bg-green-100 text-green-600",
    red: "bg-red-100 text-red-600",
    amber: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-600",
    blue: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-600",
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      className="bg-ivory rounded-2xl border border-gold/20 p-4 shadow-sm"
    >
      <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl ${colors[color]} flex items-center justify-center mb-3`}>
        {icon}
      </div>
      <p className="font-serif-display text-3xl text-ink">{value}</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-ink-light mt-1">{label}</p>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function EditGuestModal({
  guest, onSave, onClose, loading,
}: {
  guest: RSVPGuest;
  onSave: (updated: Partial<RSVPGuest>) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  loading: boolean;
}) {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    full_name: guest.full_name,
    phone: guest.phone,
    email: guest.email || "",
    attending: guest.attending,
    num_guests: guest.num_guests,
    accompanying_names: guest.accompanying_names || "",
    dietary_requirements: guest.dietary_requirements || "",
    message: guest.message || "",
  });

  const update = (field: keyof typeof formData, value: string | boolean | number) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({ ...previous, [field]: value }));
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/30 backdrop-blur-sm p-4"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <motion.div
        initial={{ scale: 0.9, y: 20 }}
        animate={{ scale: 1, y: 0 }}
        exit={{ scale: 0.9, y: 20 }}
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        className="bg-ivory rounded-2xl border border-gold/30 shadow-xl p-6 w-full max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-serif-display text-xl text-ink">Edit Guest Response</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-ink-light hover:text-ink">
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          <label className="block text-sm text-ink">
            Full Name
            <input
              value={formData.full_name}
              onChange={(e) => update("full_name", e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2"
            />
          </label>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <label className="block text-sm text-ink">
              Phone
              <input
                value={formData.phone}
                onChange={(e) => update("phone", e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="block text-sm text-ink">
              Email
              <input
                type="email"
                value={formData.email}
                onChange={(e) => update("email", e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="text-sm text-ink mb-2">Attending</p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => update("attending", true)}
                className={`py-2 rounded-lg border-2 text-sm ${
                  formData.attending ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700 border-green-300" : "border-blush-deep/20"
                }`}
              >
                Attending
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => update("attending", false)}
                className={`py-2 rounded-lg border-2 text-sm ${
                  !formData.attending ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700 border-red-300" : "border-blush-deep/20"
                }`}
              >
                Not Attending
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <label className="block text-sm text-ink">
            Number of Guests
            <input
              type="number"
              min="0"
              max="10"
              value={formData.num_guests}
              onChange={(e) => update("num_guests", Math.max(0, Math.min(10, Number(e.target.value) || 0)))}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2"
            />
          </label>

          <label className="block text-sm text-ink">
            Accompanying Names
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              value={formData.accompanying_names}
              onChange={(e) => update("accompanying_names", e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2 resize-none"
            />
          </label>

          <label className="block text-sm text-ink">
            Dietary Requirements
            <input
              value={formData.dietary_requirements}
              onChange={(e) => update("dietary_requirements", e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2"
            />
          </label>

          <label className="block text-sm text-ink">
            Message
            <textarea
              rows={3}
              value={formData.message}
              onChange={(e) => update("message", e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 bg-ivory px-3 py-2 resize-none"
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-3 mt-6">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-blush-deep/25 text-ink text-sm"
          >
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onSave(formData)}
            disabled={loading}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-lg bg-blush-deep text-ivory text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? "Saving..." : "Save Changes"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

========== FILE: app\api\admin\dashboard\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  createAdminClient,
  getCurrentAdmin,
  ID,
  Query,
} from "@/lib/appwrite-server";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

const databaseId = process.env.APPWRITE_DATABASE_ID!;
const rsvpTableId = process.env.APPWRITE_RSVP_TABLE_ID!;
const adminTableId = process.env.APPWRITE_ADMIN_TABLE_ID!;

async function getRows(tableId: string) {
  const { tables } = createAdminClient();
  const allRows: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  let offset = 0;

  while (true) {
    const result = await tables.listRows({
      databaseId,
      tableId,
      queries: [Query.limit(100), Query.offset(offset)],
    });

    allRows.push(...(result.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]));

    if (result.rows.length < 100) break;
    offset += result.rows.length;
  }

  return allRows;
}

function mapGuest(row: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return {
    id: row.$id,
    full_name: row.full_name,
    phone: row.phone,
    email: row.email ?? null,
    attending: row.attending,
    num_guests: row.num_guests,
    accompanying_names: row.accompanying_names ?? null,
    dietary_requirements: row.dietary_requirements ?? null,
    message: row.message ?? null,
    edit_token: row.edit_token,
    created_at: row.$createdAt,
    updated_at: row.$updatedAt,
  };
}

function mapStaff(row: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return {
    id: row.$id,
    user_id: row.user_id,
    email: row.email,
    full_name: row.full_name,
    role: row.role,
  };
}

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const current = await getCurrentAdmin();

    if (!current) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const guests = await getRows(rsvpTableId);
    let staff: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];

    if (current.isOwner) {
      staff = await getRows(adminTableId);
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      guests: guests.map(mapGuest),
      staff: staff.map(mapStaff),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Dashboard loading failed:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Unable to load dashboard data." },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function PATCH(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const current = await getCurrentAdmin();

    if (!current) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await request.json();
    const guestId = typeof body.id === "string" ? body.id : "";

    if (!guestId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Guest ID is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const data = {
      full_name: String(body.full_name ?? "").trim(),
      phone: String(body.phone ?? "").trim(),
      email: String(body.email ?? "").trim() || null,
      attending: Boolean(body.attending),
      num_guests: Math.max(0, Math.min(10, Number(body.num_guests) || 0)),
      accompanying_names: String(body.accompanying_names ?? ""),
      dietary_requirements: String(body.dietary_requirements ?? ""),
      message: String(body.message ?? ""),
    };

    if (!data.full_name || !data.phone) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Guest name and phone are required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { tables } = createAdminClient();

    await tables.updateRow({
      databaseId,
      tableId: rsvpTableId,
      rowId: guestId,
      data,
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Guest update failed:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Failed to update guest response." },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const current = await getCurrentAdmin();

    if (!current) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await request.json();
    const action = body.action;
    const { tables, users } = createAdminClient();

    if (action === "delete_guest") {
      const id = typeof body.id === "string" ? body.id : "";
      if (!id) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "Guest ID is required." }, { status: 400 });
      }

      await tables.deleteRow({
        databaseId,
        tableId: rsvpTableId,
        rowId: id,
      });

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
    }

    if (action === "add_staff") {
      if (!current.isOwner) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "Owner access required." }, { status: 403 });
      }

      const email = String(body.email ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
      const fullName = String(body.full_name ?? "").trim();
      const password = String(body.password ?? "");

      if (!email || !fullName || password.length < 8) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "Enter a name, email, and password of at least 8 characters." },
          { status: 400 }
        );
      }

      const user = await users.create({
        userId: ID.unique(),
        email,
        password,
        name: fullName,
      });

      try {
        await tables.createRow({
          databaseId,
          tableId: adminTableId,
          rowId: ID.unique(),
          data: {
            user_id: user.$id,
            email,
            full_name: fullName,
            role: "staff",
          },
        });
      } catch (error) {
        await users.delete({ userId: user.$id });
        throw error;
      }

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
    }

    if (action === "remove_staff") {
      if (!current.isOwner) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "Owner access required." }, { status: 403 });
      }

      const rowId = String(body.id ?? "");
      const userId = String(body.user_id ?? "");

      if (!rowId || !userId || userId === current.user.$id) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "Invalid staff account." },
          { status: 400 }
        );
      }

      const staffResult = await tables.listRows({
        databaseId,
        tableId: adminTableId,
        queries: [Query.equal("user_id", userId), Query.limit(1)],
      });

      const staff = staffResult.rows[0];

      if (!staff || staff.role === "owner") {
        return NextResponse.json(
          { error: "The owner account cannot be removed." },
          { status: 403 }
        );
      }

      await users.deleteSessions({ userId });
      await tables.deleteRow({
        databaseId,
        tableId: adminTableId,
        rowId,
      });
      await users.delete({ userId });

      return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Unknown action." }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Dashboard action failed:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "The requested action failed. Check the server logs." },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

========== FILE: app\api\admin\login\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  createAdminClient,
  createLoginClient,
  createSessionClient,
  getSessionCookieName,
  Query,
} from "@/lib/appwrite-server";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    const email =
      typeof body.email === "string" ? body.email.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
    const password =
      typeof body.password === "string" ? body.password : "";

    if (!email || !password) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Email and password are required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { account } = createLoginClient();

    const session = await account.createEmailPasswordSession({
      email,
      password,
    });

    const sessionSecret = (
      session as typeof session & { secret?: string }
    ).secret;

    if (!sessionSecret) {
      throw new Error("Appwrite did not return a session secret.");
    }

    const { tables } = createAdminClient();

    const databaseId = process.env.APPWRITE_DATABASE_ID;
    const tableId = process.env.APPWRITE_ADMIN_TABLE_ID;

    if (!databaseId || !tableId) {
      throw new Error("Admin database configuration is missing.");
    }

    const result = await tables.listRows({
      databaseId,
      tableId,
      queries: [
        Query.equal("user_id", session.userId),
        Query.limit(1),
      ],
    });

    const admin = result.rows[0];

    if (!admin) {
      // Do not leave a valid Appwrite session behind for a non-admin account.
      try {
        const { account: sessionAccount } = createSessionClient(sessionSecret);
        await sessionAccount.deleteSession({ sessionId: "current" });
      } catch (cleanupError) {
        console.error("Could not revoke non-admin login session:", cleanupError);
      }

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error: "You do not have admin access. Please contact the owner.",
        },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const response = NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      admin: {
        full_name: admin.full_name,
        role: admin.role,
      },
    });

    response.cookies.set({
      name: getSessionCookieName(),
      value: sessionSecret,
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
      sameSite: "strict",
      path: "/",
      expires: new Date(session.expire),
    });

    return response;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Admin login failed:", error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Invalid email or password. Please try again." },
      { status: 401 }
    );
  }
}


========== FILE: app\api\admin\logout\route.ts ==========
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  createSessionClient,
  getSessionCookieName,
} from "@/lib/appwrite-server";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

export async function POST() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const cookieName = getSessionCookieName();
  const secret = cookieStore.get(cookieName)?.value;

  if (secret) {
    try {
      const { account } = createSessionClient(secret);
      await account.deleteSession({ sessionId: "current" });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Appwrite logout:", error);
    }
  }

  const response = NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  response.cookies.set({
    name: cookieName,
    value: "",
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    sameSite: "strict",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: 0,
  });

  return response;
}

export const runtime = "nodejs";

export async function GET() {

  if (!currentAdmin) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { authenticated: false },
      { status: 401 }
    );
  }

  return NextResponse.json({

========== FILE: app\api\guests\register\route.ts ==========

import { randomBytes, createHash } from "crypto";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";
import { createAdminClient, ID } from "@/lib/appwrite-server";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

const schema = z.object({
  full_name: z.string().trim().min(2).max(200),
  phone: z.string().trim().max(40).optional().default(""),
  email: z.union([z.string().trim().email().max(320), z.literal("")]).optional().default(""),
  companion_name: z.string().trim().max(200).optional().default(""),
});

const attempts = new Map<string, number[]>();

function rateLimited(ip: string) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const recent = (attempts.get(ip) ?? []).filter((time) => now - time < 60 * 60 * 1000);
  if (recent.length >= 8) {
    attempts.set(ip, recent);
    return true;
  }
  recent.push(now);
  attempts.set(ip, recent);
  return false;
}

function makeCode() {
  return randomBytes(8).toString("hex").toUpperCase();
}

function makeToken() {
  return randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
}

function normalizeName(value: string) {
  return value.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ").toLocaleLowerCase();
}

function fingerprint(value: string) {
  return createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const forwarded = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for");
    const ip = forwarded?.split(",")[0]?.trim() || "unknown";

    if (rateLimited(ip)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Too many registration attempts. Please try again later." },
        { status: 429 }
      );
    }

    const parsed = schema.safeParse(await request.json());

    if (!parsed.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Please check your name, phone number, and email address." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const input = parsed.data;
    const names = [input.full_name, input.companion_name]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((name) => name.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " "));

    if (
      names.length === 2 &&
      normalizeName(names[0]) === normalizeName(names[1])
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "The guest and companion must have different names." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { tables } = createAdminClient();
    const databaseId = process.env.APPWRITE_DATABASE_ID!;
    const tableId = process.env.APPWRITE_INVITATIONS_TABLE_ID!;

    if (!databaseId || !tableId) {
      throw new Error("Invitation database configuration is missing.");
    }

    const created: string[] = [];
    const guests: { full_name: string; invitation_url: string }[] = [];

    try {
      for (const [index, fullName] of names.entries()) {
        const token = makeToken();

        const record = {
          full_name: fullName,
          phone: index === 0 ? input.phone : "",
          email: index === 0 ? input.email : "",
          invitation_code: makeCode(),
          qr_token: token,
          status: "responded",
          attending: true,
          num_guests: 0,
          checked_in: false,
          created_by: "public-registration",
          message: `registration_fingerprint:${fingerprint(normalizeName(fullName))}`,
        };

        const row = await tables.createRow({
          databaseId,
          tableId,
          rowId: ID.unique(),
          data: record,
        });

        created.push(row.$id);
        guests.push({
          full_name: fullName,
          invitation_url: `/invitation/${token}`,
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      for (const rowId of created) {
        try {
          await tables.deleteRow({ databaseId, tableId, rowId });
        } catch {
          // Best-effort rollback.
        }
      }
      throw error;
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, guests }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Guest registration failed:", error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Registration could not be completed. Please try again." },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}


========== FILE: app\api\guests\[id]\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createAdminClient, getCurrentAdmin } from "@/lib/appwrite-server";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

type RouteContext = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

export async function PATCH(
  request: NextRequest,
  context: RouteContext
) {
  try {
    const current = await getCurrentAdmin();

    if (!current || !current.isOwner) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Owner access required." },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    const { id } = await context.params;
    const body = await request.json();

    if (!id) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Guest ID is required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const action = body.action;

    if (action !== "revoke" && action !== "restore") {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Invalid invitation action." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const databaseId = process.env.APPWRITE_DATABASE_ID;
    const tableId = process.env.APPWRITE_INVITATIONS_TABLE_ID;

    if (!databaseId || !tableId) {
      throw new Error("Invitation database configuration is missing.");
    }

    const { tables } = createAdminClient();

    const existing = await tables.getRow({
      databaseId,
      tableId,
      rowId: id,
    });

    if (action === "restore" && existing.checked_in === true) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "This guest has already checked in and cannot be restored automatically." },
        { status: 409 }
      );
    }

    await tables.updateRow({
      databaseId,
      tableId,
      rowId: id,
      data: {
        status: action === "revoke" ? "revoked" : "responded",
      },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      status: action === "revoke" ? "revoked" : "responded",
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Invitation status update failed:", error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: "Unable to update invitation status." },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}


========== FILE: app\api\rsvp\route.ts ==========

import { randomBytes } from 'crypto';
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { createAdminClient, ID, Query } from '@/lib/appwrite-server';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

const rsvpSchema = z.object({
  invitation_code: z.string().trim().max(16).optional(),
  full_name: z.string().trim().min(2).max(200),
  phone: z.string().trim().min(7).max(40),
  email: z.string().trim().email().or(z.literal('')).optional(),
  attending: z.boolean(),
  num_guests: z.number().int().min(0).max(10),
  accompanying_names: z.string().max(2000).optional(),
  dietary_requirements: z.string().max(2000).optional(),
  message: z.string().max(5000).optional(),
});

const updateSchema = rsvpSchema.omit({ invitation_code: true });

type RSVPInput = z.infer<typeof updateSchema>;

function getDatabaseConfig() {
  const databaseId = process.env.APPWRITE_DATABASE_ID;
  const rsvpTableId = process.env.APPWRITE_RSVP_TABLE_ID;
  const invitationsTableId = process.env.APPWRITE_INVITATIONS_TABLE_ID;

  if (!databaseId || !rsvpTableId || !invitationsTableId) {
    throw new Error('RSVP database configuration is incomplete.');
  }

  return { databaseId, rsvpTableId, invitationsTableId };
}

function normalizeName(value: string) {
  return value.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
}

function normalizePhone(value: string) {
  return value.replace(/\D/g, '');
}

function validToken(token: unknown): token is string {
  return typeof token === 'string' && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(token);
}

function publicRsvp(row: Record<string, any>) {
  return {
    full_name: row.full_name,
    phone: row.phone,
    email: row.email || '',
    attending: row.attending,
    num_guests: row.num_guests || 0,
    accompanying_names: row.accompanying_names || '',
    dietary_requirements: row.dietary_requirements || '',
    message: row.message || '',
  };
}

async function findRsvpByToken(
  tables: any,
  databaseId: string,
  rsvpTableId: string,
  token: string
) {
  const result = await tables.listRows({
    databaseId,
    tableId: rsvpTableId,
    queries: [Query.equal('edit_token', token), Query.limit(1)],
  });

  return result.rows?.[0] || null;
}

async function rollbackTransaction(tables: any, transactionId: string) {
  try {
    await tables.updateTransaction({
      transactionId,
      rollback: true,
    });
  } catch {
    // A transaction that has already failed or expired may not need rollback.
  }
}

function errorResponse(error: unknown) {
  const code =
    typeof error === 'object' && error !== null && 'code' in error
      ? Number((error as { code: unknown }).code)
      : 0;

  console.error('RSVP API error:', error);

  if (code === 409) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          'This invitation has just been used or updated. Please refresh and try again.',
      },
      { status: 409 }
    );
  }

  return NextResponse.json(
    { error: 'Something went wrong. Please try again later.' },
    { status: 500 }
  );
}

/**
 * GET: Retrieve an existing RSVP using its private edit token.
 */
export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const token = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('token');

    if (!validToken(token)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'A valid response code is required.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { databaseId, rsvpTableId } = getDatabaseConfig();
    const { tables } = createAdminClient();

    const row = await findRsvpByToken(
      tables,
      databaseId,
      rsvpTableId,
      token
    );

    if (!row) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'No response was found with that code.' },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ rsvp: publicRsvp(row) });
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error);
  }
}

/**
 * POST: Create an RSVP only for an existing, preapproved invitation.
 */
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);
    const parsed = rsvpSchema.safeParse(body);

    if (!parsed.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error: 'Please check your RSVP details and try again.',
          details: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const input = parsed.data;
    const invitationCode = input.invitation_code
      ?.trim()
      .toUpperCase();

    if (!invitationCode) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Your invitation code is required.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { databaseId, rsvpTableId, invitationsTableId } =
      getDatabaseConfig();

    const { tables } = createAdminClient();

    const invitationResult = await tables.listRows({
      databaseId,
      tableId: invitationsTableId,
      queries: [
        Query.equal('invitation_code', invitationCode),
        Query.limit(1),
      ],
    });

    const invitation = invitationResult.rows?.[0];

    if (!invitation) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            'We could not find that invitation code. Please check your invitation.',
        },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    if (
      normalizeName(input.full_name) !==
        normalizeName(invitation.full_name || '') ||
      normalizePhone(input.phone) !==
        normalizePhone(invitation.phone || '')
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            'Your name or phone number does not match the invitation. Please enter the details provided by the couple.',
        },
        { status: 403 }
      );
    }

    if (invitation.status !== 'invited') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            invitation.status === 'responded'
              ? 'An RSVP has already been submitted for this invitation.'
              : 'This invitation is not currently available for RSVP.',
        },
        { status: 409 }
      );
    }

    if (invitation.rsvp_id) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            'An RSVP is already associated with this invitation. Please use your response code to edit it.',
        },
        { status: 409 }
      );
    }

    const editToken = randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
    const qrToken = randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
    const rsvpId = ID.unique();

    const {
      invitation_code: _invitationCode,
      ...submittedFields
    } = input;

    const rsvpData = {
      ...submittedFields,
      email: input.email || '',
      num_guests: input.attending ? input.num_guests : 0,
      accompanying_names: input.accompanying_names || '',
      dietary_requirements: input.dietary_requirements || '',
      message: input.message || '',
      edit_token: editToken,
    };

    const transaction = await tables.createTransaction();

    try {
      // Stage creation of the RSVP.
      await tables.createRow({
        databaseId,
        tableId: rsvpTableId,
        rowId: rsvpId,
        data: rsvpData,
        transactionId: transaction.$id,
      });

      // Stage the invitation claim in the same transaction.
      await tables.updateRow({
        databaseId,
        tableId: invitationsTableId,
        rowId: invitation.$id,
        data: {
          status: 'responded',
          qr_token: qrToken,
          attending: input.attending,
          num_guests: input.attending ? input.num_guests : 0,
          accompanying_names: input.accompanying_names || '',
          dietary_requirements: input.dietary_requirements || '',
          message: input.message || '',
          rsvp_id: rsvpId,
        },
        transactionId: transaction.$id,
      });

      // Both operations become permanent together.
      await tables.updateTransaction({
        transactionId: transaction.$id,
        commit: true,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      await rollbackTransaction(tables, transaction.$id);
      throw error;
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        edit_token: editToken,
        qr_token: qrToken,
        rsvp: publicRsvp(rsvpData),
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error);
  }
}

/**
 * PATCH: Edit an existing RSVP using its private edit token.
 */
export async function PATCH(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);

    if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Invalid request.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const token = (body as Record<string, unknown>).edit_token;

    if (!validToken(token)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'A valid response code is required.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const parsed = updateSchema.safeParse(body);

    if (!parsed.success) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error: 'Please check your RSVP details and try again.',
          details: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { databaseId, rsvpTableId, invitationsTableId } =
      getDatabaseConfig();

    const { tables } = createAdminClient();

    const existing = await findRsvpByToken(
      tables,
      databaseId,
      rsvpTableId,
      token
    );

    if (!existing) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'No response was found with that code.' },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    const input: RSVPInput = parsed.data;

    const updateData = {
      ...input,
      email: input.email || '',
      num_guests: input.attending ? input.num_guests : 0,
      accompanying_names: input.accompanying_names || '',
      dietary_requirements: input.dietary_requirements || '',
      message: input.message || '',
    };

    const invitationResult = await tables.listRows({
      databaseId,
      tableId: invitationsTableId,
      queries: [
        Query.equal('rsvp_id', existing.$id),
        Query.limit(1),
      ],
    });

    const invitation = invitationResult.rows?.[0] || null;

    const transaction = await tables.createTransaction();

    try {
      await tables.updateRow({
        databaseId,
        tableId: rsvpTableId,
        rowId: existing.$id,
        data: updateData,
        transactionId: transaction.$id,
      });

      if (invitation && invitation.status === 'responded') {
        await tables.updateRow({
          databaseId,
          tableId: invitationsTableId,
          rowId: invitation.$id,
          data: {
            attending: input.attending,
            num_guests: input.attending ? input.num_guests : 0,
            accompanying_names: input.accompanying_names || '',
            dietary_requirements: input.dietary_requirements || '',
            message: input.message || '',
          },
          transactionId: transaction.$id,
        });
      }

      await tables.updateTransaction({
        transactionId: transaction.$id,
        commit: true,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      await rollbackTransaction(tables, transaction.$id);
      throw error;
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      edit_token: token,
      rsvp: publicRsvp(updateData),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return errorResponse(error);
  }
}


========== FILE: app\invitation\[token]\page.tsx ==========

import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { Query } from "node-appwrite";
import { createAdminClient } from "@/lib/appwrite-server";
import InvitationQRCode from "@/components/InvitationQRCode";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

type Props = {
  params: Promise<{ token: string }>;
};

type InvitationRecord = {
  $id: string;
  full_name: string;
  status: string;
  attending: boolean;
  checked_in: boolean;
};

export default async function InvitationPage({ params }: Props) {
  const { token } = await params;

  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i.test(token)) {
    notFound();
  }

  const databaseId = process.env.APPWRITE_DATABASE_ID;
  const tableId = process.env.APPWRITE_INVITATIONS_TABLE_ID;

  if (!databaseId || !tableId) {
    throw new Error("Invitation database configuration is missing.");
  }

  const { tables } = createAdminClient();

  const result = await tables.listRows({
    databaseId,
    tableId,
    queries: [
      Query.equal("qr_token", token),
      Query.limit(1),
    ],
  });

  const invitation = result.rows[0] as unknown as
    | InvitationRecord
    | undefined;

  if (!invitation) {
    notFound();
  }

  if (invitation.status === "revoked") {
    return (
      <main className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#f8f4ec] p-6">
        <section className="max-w-md w-full text-center bg-white p-10 rounded-2xl shadow-xl">
          <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl text-[#8d3030]">
            Invitation Deactivated
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-4 text-gray-600">
            This invitation is no longer active. Please contact the couple.
          </p>
        </section>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (invitation.status !== "responded" || !invitation.attending) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#f8f4ec] p-6">
      <section className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-xl">
        <p className="uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-xs text-[#aa8954]">
          Wedding Invitation
        </p>

        <h1 className="mt-5 font-serif text-4xl text-[#44372c]">
          Betslot & Amanuel
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-5 text-lg text-gray-700">
          You are warmly invited, {invitation.full_name}.
        </p>

        <p className="mt-3 text-gray-600">
          19 November 2028 · 8:00 PM
        </p>

        <p className="mt-1 text-gray-600">
          Friendship Park · Addis Ababa, Ethiopia
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
          <InvitationQRCode token={token} />
        </div>

        <p className="mt-5 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Please present this QR code at the entrance.
        </p>
      </section>

  );
}


========== FILE: components\Countdown.tsx ==========
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

/* Target: 19 November 2028 at 8:00 PM in Addis Ababa (UTC+3) = 17:00 UTC */
const TARGET_UTC = new Date('2028-11-19T17:00:00Z').getTime();

interface TimeLeft {
  days: number;
  hours: number;
  minutes: number;
  seconds: number;
}

function calculateTimeLeft(): TimeLeft {
  const now = Date.now();
  const diff = TARGET_UTC - now;

  if (diff <= 0) {
    return { days: 0, hours: 0, minutes: 0, seconds: 0 };
  }

  return {
    days: Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)),
    hours: Math.floor((diff / (1000 * 60 * 60)) % 24),
    minutes: Math.floor((diff / (1000 * 60)) % 60),
    seconds: Math.floor((diff / 1000) % 60),
  };
}

export function Countdown() {
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState<TimeLeft>({
    days: 0,
    hours: 0,
    minutes: 0,
    seconds: 0,
  });
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
    setTimeLeft(calculateTimeLeft());

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setTimeLeft(calculateTimeLeft());
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const units: { label: string; value: number }[] = [
    { label: 'Days', value: timeLeft.days },
    { label: 'Hours', value: timeLeft.hours },
    { label: 'Minutes', value: timeLeft.minutes },
    { label: 'Seconds', value: timeLeft.seconds },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3 md:gap-6">
      {units.map((unit, i) => (
        <motion.div
          key={unit.label}
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
          whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          viewport={{ once: true }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: i * 0.1 }}
          className="relative"
        >
          <div className="w-20 h-24 md:w-28 md:h-32 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-ivory to-blush-light border border-gold/30 shadow-md flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
            {/* Watercolor wash */}
            <div
              className="absolute inset-0 opacity-30"
              style={{
                background:
                  'radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 60% at 50% 30%, hsl(340 30% 88%), transparent)',
              }}
            />
            <motion.div
              key={mounted ? unit.value : 'init'}
              initial={{ y: -20, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
              className="relative font-serif-display text-3xl md:text-5xl font-medium text-blush-deep tabular-nums"
            >
              {mounted ? String(unit.value).padStart(2, '0') : '00'}
            </motion.div>
            <p className="relative font-body text-[10px] md:text-xs tracking-[0.2em] uppercase text-ink-light mt-1">
              {unit.label}
            </p>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
